+namespace {
+
+// Matrices are stored as dist[destination * stride + source].  Threads vary
+// source along x so every global-memory matrix load/store is coalesced.
+__global__ void floydPivotKernel(unsigned int* __restrict__ dist,
+                                 unsigned int* __restrict__ path,
+                                 const size_t stride,
+                                 const unsigned int pivotTile) {
+    __shared__ unsigned int tile[TILE_SIZE][TILE_SIZE + 1];
+
+    const unsigned int source = pivotTile * TILE_SIZE + threadIdx.x;
+    const unsigned int destination = pivotTile * TILE_SIZE + threadIdx.y;
+    const size_t element = static_cast<size_t>(destination) * stride + source;
+
+    tile[threadIdx.y][threadIdx.x] = dist[element];
+    unsigned int pathValue = path[element];
+    __syncthreads();
+
+    #pragma unroll
+    for (unsigned int k = 0; k < TILE_SIZE; ++k) {
+        const unsigned int candidate = tile[k][threadIdx.x] + tile[threadIdx.y][k];
+        if (candidate < tile[threadIdx.y][threadIdx.x]) {
+            tile[threadIdx.y][threadIdx.x] = candidate;
+            pathValue = pivotTile * TILE_SIZE + k;
+        }
+        __syncthreads();
+    }
+
+    dist[element] = tile[threadIdx.y][threadIdx.x];
+    path[element] = pathValue;
+}
+
+// Updates both the pivot column and pivot row.  The second grid dimension
+// selects D(other, pivot) or D(pivot, other), respectively.
+__global__ void floydPivotRowColumnKernel(unsigned int* __restrict__ dist,
+                                          unsigned int* __restrict__ path,
+                                          const size_t stride,
+                                          const unsigned int pivotTile) {
+    __shared__ unsigned int pivot[TILE_SIZE][TILE_SIZE + 1];
+    __shared__ unsigned int current[TILE_SIZE][TILE_SIZE + 1];
 
+    unsigned int otherTile = blockIdx.x;
+    if (otherTile >= pivotTile) {
+        ++otherTile;
+    }
+    const bool pivotRow = blockIdx.y != 0;
+    const unsigned int sourceTile = pivotRow ? pivotTile : otherTile;
+    const unsigned int destinationTile = pivotRow ? otherTile : pivotTile;
+    const unsigned int source = sourceTile * TILE_SIZE + threadIdx.x;
+    const unsigned int destination = destinationTile * TILE_SIZE + threadIdx.y;
+    const unsigned int pivotSource = pivotTile * TILE_SIZE + threadIdx.x;
+    const unsigned int pivotDestination = pivotTile * TILE_SIZE + threadIdx.y;
+    const size_t element = static_cast<size_t>(destination) * stride + source;
+    const size_t pivotElement = static_cast<size_t>(pivotDestination) * stride + pivotSource;
+
+    current[threadIdx.y][threadIdx.x] = dist[element];
+    pivot[threadIdx.y][threadIdx.x] = dist[pivotElement];
+    unsigned int pathValue = path[element];
+    __syncthreads();
+
+    #pragma unroll
+    for (unsigned int k = 0; k < TILE_SIZE; ++k) {
+        const unsigned int left = pivotRow ? pivot[k][threadIdx.x]
+                                           : current[k][threadIdx.x];
+        const unsigned int right = pivotRow ? current[threadIdx.y][k]
+                                            : pivot[threadIdx.y][k];
+        const unsigned int candidate = left + right;
+        if (candidate < current[threadIdx.y][threadIdx.x]) {
+            current[threadIdx.y][threadIdx.x] = candidate;
+            pathValue = pivotTile * TILE_SIZE + k;
+        }
+        __syncthreads();
+    }
+
+    dist[element] = current[threadIdx.y][threadIdx.x];
+    path[element] = pathValue;
+}
+
+// The remaining tiles are independent once the pivot row and column have
+// been closed.  This phase exposes almost all of the benchmark's parallelism.
+__global__ void floydRemainingKernel(unsigned int* __restrict__ dist,
+                                     unsigned int* __restrict__ path,
+                                     const size_t stride,
+                                     const unsigned int pivotTile) {
+    __shared__ unsigned int left[TILE_SIZE][TILE_SIZE + 1];
+    __shared__ unsigned int right[TILE_SIZE][TILE_SIZE + 1];
+
+    unsigned int sourceTile = blockIdx.x;
+    unsigned int destinationTile = blockIdx.y;
+    if (sourceTile >= pivotTile) {
+        ++sourceTile;
+    }
+    if (destinationTile >= pivotTile) {
+        ++destinationTile;
+    }
+
+    const unsigned int source = sourceTile * TILE_SIZE + threadIdx.x;
+    const unsigned int destination = destinationTile * TILE_SIZE + threadIdx.y;
+    const unsigned int pivotSource = pivotTile * TILE_SIZE + threadIdx.x;
+    const unsigned int pivotDestination = pivotTile * TILE_SIZE + threadIdx.y;
+    const size_t element = static_cast<size_t>(destination) * stride + source;
+    const size_t leftElement = static_cast<size_t>(pivotDestination) * stride + source;
+    const size_t rightElement = static_cast<size_t>(destination) * stride + pivotSource;
+
+    left[threadIdx.y][threadIdx.x] = dist[leftElement];
+    right[threadIdx.y][threadIdx.x] = dist[rightElement];
+    unsigned int distance = dist[element];
+    unsigned int pathValue = path[element];
+    __syncthreads();
+
+    #pragma unroll
+    for (unsigned int k = 0; k < TILE_SIZE; ++k) {
+        const unsigned int candidate = left[k][threadIdx.x] + right[threadIdx.y][k];
+        if (candidate < distance) {
+            distance = candidate;
+            pathValue = pivotTile * TILE_SIZE + k;
+        }
+    }
+
+    dist[element] = distance;
+    path[element] = pathValue;
+}
+
+float floydWarshallCuda(std::vector<unsigned int>& dist,
+                        std::vector<unsigned int>& path,
+                        const size_t numNodes) {
+    if (numNodes == 0) {
+        return 0.0F;
+    }
+
+    const size_t paddedNodes = ((numNodes + TILE_SIZE - 1) / TILE_SIZE) * TILE_SIZE;
+    if (paddedNodes > std::numeric_limits<unsigned int>::max()) {
+        std::fprintf(stderr, "CUDA Floyd-Warshall supports at most %u nodes\\n",
+                     std::numeric_limits<unsigned int>::max());
+        std::exit(EXIT_FAILURE);
+    }
+
+    const size_t paddedElements = paddedNodes * paddedNodes;
+    std::vector<unsigned int> paddedDist(paddedElements, INF);
+    std::vector<unsigned int> paddedPath(paddedElements, 0);
+    for (size_t destination = 0; destination < numNodes; ++destination) {
+        std::copy_n(dist.data() + destination * numNodes, numNodes,
+                    paddedDist.data() + destination * paddedNodes);
+        std::copy_n(path.data() + destination * numNodes, numNodes,
+                    paddedPath.data() + destination * paddedNodes);
+    }
+
+    unsigned int* deviceDist = nullptr;
+    unsigned int* devicePath = nullptr;
+    const size_t bytes = paddedElements * sizeof(unsigned int);
+    CUDA_CHECK(cudaMalloc(&deviceDist, bytes));
+    CUDA_CHECK(cudaMalloc(&devicePath, bytes));
+    CUDA_CHECK(cudaMemcpy(deviceDist, paddedDist.data(), bytes, cudaMemcpyHostToDevice));
+    CUDA_CHECK(cudaMemcpy(devicePath, paddedPath.data(), bytes, cudaMemcpyHostToDevice));
+
+    cudaEvent_t start{};
+    cudaEvent_t stop{};
+    CUDA_CHECK(cudaEventCreate(&start));
+    CUDA_CHECK(cudaEventCreate(&stop));
+
+    const unsigned int tileCount = static_cast<unsigned int>(paddedNodes / TILE_SIZE);
+    const dim3 threads(TILE_SIZE, TILE_SIZE);
+    CUDA_CHECK(cudaEventRecord(start));
+    for (unsigned int pivotTile = 0; pivotTile < tileCount; ++pivotTile) {
+        floydPivotKernel<<<1, threads>>>(deviceDist, devicePath, paddedNodes, pivotTile);
+        CUDA_CHECK(cudaPeekAtLastError());
+
+        if (tileCount > 1) {
+            floydPivotRowColumnKernel<<<dim3(tileCount - 1, 2), threads>>>(
+                deviceDist, devicePath, paddedNodes, pivotTile);
+            CUDA_CHECK(cudaPeekAtLastError());
+            floydRemainingKernel<<<dim3(tileCount - 1, tileCount - 1), threads>>>(
+                deviceDist, devicePath, paddedNodes, pivotTile);
+            CUDA_CHECK(cudaPeekAtLastError());
+        }
+    }
+    CUDA_CHECK(cudaEventRecord(stop));
+    CUDA_CHECK(cudaEventSynchronize(stop));
+
+    float elapsedMilliseconds = 0.0F;
+    CUDA_CHECK(cudaEventElapsedTime(&elapsedMilliseconds, start, stop));
+
+    CUDA_CHECK(cudaMemcpy2D(dist.data(), numNodes * sizeof(unsigned int),
+                            deviceDist, paddedNodes * sizeof(unsigned int),
+                            numNodes * sizeof(unsigned int), numNodes,
+                            cudaMemcpyDeviceToHost));
+    CUDA_CHECK(cudaMemcpy2D(path.data(), numNodes * sizeof(unsigned int),
+                            devicePath, paddedNodes * sizeof(unsigned int),
+                            numNodes * sizeof(unsigned int), numNodes,
+                            cudaMemcpyDeviceToHost));
+
+    CUDA_CHECK(cudaEventDestroy(start));
+    CUDA_CHECK(cudaEventDestroy(stop));
+    CUDA_CHECK(cudaFree(deviceDist));
+    CUDA_CHECK(cudaFree(devicePath));
+    return elapsedMilliseconds;
+}
+

 #include <algorithm>
-#include <chrono>
 #include <cmath>
 #include <cstdio>
 #include <cstdlib>
 #include <cstring>
+#include <limits>
 #include <vector>
 
+#include <cuda_runtime.h>
+
 #include "../common/results_output.hpp"
 
 constexpr unsigned int INF = 1000000000;
 constexpr unsigned int MAX_DISTANCE = 200;
+constexpr unsigned int TILE_SIZE = 16;
+
+#define CUDA_CHECK(call)                                                        \
+    do {                                                                        \
+        const cudaError_t error_ = (call);                                     \
+        if (error_ != cudaSuccess) {                                           \
+            std::fprintf(stderr, "CUDA error at %s:%d: %s\\n", __FILE__,     \
+                         __LINE__, cudaGetErrorString(error_));                \
+            std::exit(EXIT_FAILURE);                                           \
+        }                                                                       \
+    } while (false)
+
+namespace {
+
+// Matrices are stored as dist[destination * stride + source].  Threads vary
+// source along x so every global-memory matrix load/store is coalesced.
+__global__ void floydPivotKernel(unsigned int* __restrict__ dist,
+                                 unsigned int* __restrict__ path,
+                                 const size_t stride,
+                                 const unsigned int pivotTile) {
+    __shared__ unsigned int tile[TILE_SIZE][TILE_SIZE + 1];
+
+    const unsigned int source = pivotTile * TILE_SIZE + threadIdx.x;
+    const unsigned int destination = pivotTile * TILE_SIZE + threadIdx.y;
+    const size_t element = static_cast<size_t>(destination) * stride + source;
+
+    tile[threadIdx.y][threadIdx.x] = dist[element];
+    unsigned int pathValue = path[element];
+    __syncthreads();
+
+    #pragma unroll
+    for (unsigned int k = 0; k < TILE_SIZE; ++k) {
+        const unsigned int candidate = tile[k][threadIdx.x] + tile[threadIdx.y][k];
+        if (candidate < tile[threadIdx.y][threadIdx.x]) {
+            tile[threadIdx.y][threadIdx.x] = candidate;
+            pathValue = pivotTile * TILE_SIZE + k;
+        }
+        __syncthreads();
+    }
+
+    dist[element] = tile[threadIdx.y][threadIdx.x];
+    path[element] = pathValue;
+}
+
+// Updates both the pivot column and pivot row.  The second grid dimension
+// selects D(other, pivot) or D(pivot, other), respectively.
+__global__ void floydPivotRowColumnKernel(unsigned int* __restrict__ dist,
+                                          unsigned int* __restrict__ path,
+                                          const size_t stride,
+                                          const unsigned int pivotTile) {
+    __shared__ unsigned int pivot[TILE_SIZE][TILE_SIZE + 1];
+    __shared__ unsigned int current[TILE_SIZE][TILE_SIZE + 1];
 
+    unsigned int otherTile = blockIdx.x;
+    if (otherTile >= pivotTile) {
+        ++otherTile;
+    }
+    const bool pivotRow = blockIdx.y != 0;
+    const unsigned int sourceTile = pivotRow ? pivotTile : otherTile;
+    const unsigned int destinationTile = pivotRow ? otherTile : pivotTile;
+    const unsigned int source = sourceTile * TILE_SIZE + threadIdx.x;
+    const unsigned int destination = destinationTile * TILE_SIZE + threadIdx.y;
+    const unsigned int pivotSource = pivotTile * TILE_SIZE + threadIdx.x;
+    const unsigned int pivotDestination = pivotTile * TILE_SIZE + threadIdx.y;
+    const size_t element = static_cast<size_t>(destination) * stride + source;
+    const size_t pivotElement = static_cast<size_t>(pivotDestination) * stride + pivotSource;
+
+    current[threadIdx.y][threadIdx.x] = dist[element];
+    pivot[threadIdx.y][threadIdx.x] = dist[pivotElement];
+    unsigned int pathValue = path[element];
+    __syncthreads();
+
+    #pragma unroll
+    for (unsigned int k = 0; k < TILE_SIZE; ++k) {
+        const unsigned int left = pivotRow ? pivot[k][threadIdx.x]
+                                           : current[k][threadIdx.x];
+        const unsigned int right = pivotRow ? current[threadIdx.y][k]
+                                            : pivot[threadIdx.y][k];
+        const unsigned int candidate = left + right;
+        if (candidate < current[threadIdx.y][threadIdx.x]) {
+            current[threadIdx.y][threadIdx.x] = candidate;
+            pathValue = pivotTile * TILE_SIZE + k;
+        }
+        __syncthreads();
+    }
+
+    dist[element] = current[threadIdx.y][threadIdx.x];
+    path[element] = pathValue;
+}
+
+// The remaining tiles are independent once the pivot row and column have
+// been closed.  This phase exposes almost all of the benchmark's parallelism.
+__global__ void floydRemainingKernel(unsigned int* __restrict__ dist,
+                                     unsigned int* __restrict__ path,
+                                     const size_t stride,
+                                     const unsigned int pivotTile) {
+    __shared__ unsigned int left[TILE_SIZE][TILE_SIZE + 1];
+    __shared__ unsigned int right[TILE_SIZE][TILE_SIZE + 1];
+
+    unsigned int sourceTile = blockIdx.x;
+    unsigned int destinationTile = blockIdx.y;
+    if (sourceTile >= pivotTile) {
+        ++sourceTile;
+    }
+    if (destinationTile >= pivotTile) {
+        ++destinationTile;
+    }
+
+    const unsigned int source = sourceTile * TILE_SIZE + threadIdx.x;
+    const unsigned int destination = destinationTile * TILE_SIZE + threadIdx.y;
+    const unsigned int pivotSource = pivotTile * TILE_SIZE + threadIdx.x;
+    const unsigned int pivotDestination = pivotTile * TILE_SIZE + threadIdx.y;
+    const size_t element = static_cast<size_t>(destination) * stride + source;
+    const size_t leftElement = static_cast<size_t>(pivotDestination) * stride + source;
+    const size_t rightElement = static_cast<size_t>(destination) * stride + pivotSource;
+
+    left[threadIdx.y][threadIdx.x] = dist[leftElement];
+    right[threadIdx.y][threadIdx.x] = dist[rightElement];
+    unsigned int distance = dist[element];
+    unsigned int pathValue = path[element];
+    __syncthreads();
+
+    #pragma unroll
+    for (unsigned int k = 0; k < TILE_SIZE; ++k) {
+        const unsigned int candidate = left[k][threadIdx.x] + right[threadIdx.y][k];
+        if (candidate < distance) {
+            distance = candidate;
+            pathValue = pivotTile * TILE_SIZE + k;
+        }
+    }
+
+    dist[element] = distance;
+    path[element] = pathValue;
+}
+
+float floydWarshallCuda(std::vector<unsigned int>& dist,
+                        std::vector<unsigned int>& path,
+                        const size_t numNodes) {
+    if (numNodes == 0) {
+        return 0.0F;
+    }
+
+    const size_t paddedNodes = ((numNodes + TILE_SIZE - 1) / TILE_SIZE) * TILE_SIZE;
+    if (paddedNodes > std::numeric_limits<unsigned int>::max()) {
+        std::fprintf(stderr, "CUDA Floyd-Warshall supports at most %u nodes\\n",
+                     std::numeric_limits<unsigned int>::max());
+        std::exit(EXIT_FAILURE);
+    }
+
+    const size_t paddedElements = paddedNodes * paddedNodes;
+    std::vector<unsigned int> paddedDist(paddedElements, INF);
+    std::vector<unsigned int> paddedPath(paddedElements, 0);
+    for (size_t destination = 0; destination < numNodes; ++destination) {
+        std::copy_n(dist.data() + destination * numNodes, numNodes,
+                    paddedDist.data() + destination * paddedNodes);
+        std::copy_n(path.data() + destination * numNodes, numNodes,
+                    paddedPath.data() + destination * paddedNodes);
+    }
+
+    unsigned int* deviceDist = nullptr;
+    unsigned int* devicePath = nullptr;
+    const size_t bytes = paddedElements * sizeof(unsigned int);
+    CUDA_CHECK(cudaMalloc(&deviceDist, bytes));
+    CUDA_CHECK(cudaMalloc(&devicePath, bytes));
+    CUDA_CHECK(cudaMemcpy(deviceDist, paddedDist.data(), bytes, cudaMemcpyHostToDevice));
+    CUDA_CHECK(cudaMemcpy(devicePath, paddedPath.data(), bytes, cudaMemcpyHostToDevice));
+
+    cudaEvent_t start{};
+    cudaEvent_t stop{};
+    CUDA_CHECK(cudaEventCreate(&start));
+    CUDA_CHECK(cudaEventCreate(&stop));
+
+    const unsigned int tileCount = static_cast<unsigned int>(paddedNodes / TILE_SIZE);
+    const dim3 threads(TILE_SIZE, TILE_SIZE);
+    CUDA_CHECK(cudaEventRecord(start));
+    for (unsigned int pivotTile = 0; pivotTile < tileCount; ++pivotTile) {
+        floydPivotKernel<<<1, threads>>>(deviceDist, devicePath, paddedNodes, pivotTile);
+        CUDA_CHECK(cudaPeekAtLastError());
+
+        if (tileCount > 1) {
+            floydPivotRowColumnKernel<<<dim3(tileCount - 1, 2), threads>>>(
+                deviceDist, devicePath, paddedNodes, pivotTile);
+            CUDA_CHECK(cudaPeekAtLastError());
+            floydRemainingKernel<<<dim3(tileCount - 1, tileCount - 1), threads>>>(
+                deviceDist, devicePath, paddedNodes, pivotTile);
+            CUDA_CHECK(cudaPeekAtLastError());
+        }
+    }
+    CUDA_CHECK(cudaEventRecord(stop));
+    CUDA_CHECK(cudaEventSynchronize(stop));
+
+    float elapsedMilliseconds = 0.0F;
+    CUDA_CHECK(cudaEventElapsedTime(&elapsedMilliseconds, start, stop));
+
+    CUDA_CHECK(cudaMemcpy2D(dist.data(), numNodes * sizeof(unsigned int),
+                            deviceDist, paddedNodes * sizeof(unsigned int),
+                            numNodes * sizeof(unsigned int), numNodes,
+                            cudaMemcpyDeviceToHost));
+    CUDA_CHECK(cudaMemcpy2D(path.data(), numNodes * sizeof(unsigned int),
+                            devicePath, paddedNodes * sizeof(unsigned int),
+                            numNodes * sizeof(unsigned int), numNodes,
+                            cudaMemcpyDeviceToHost));
+
+    CUDA_CHECK(cudaEventDestroy(start));
+    CUDA_CHECK(cudaEventDestroy(stop));
+    CUDA_CHECK(cudaFree(deviceDist));
+    CUDA_CHECK(cudaFree(devicePath));
+    return elapsedMilliseconds;
+}
+
+}  // namespace
+
 // Index calculation for flattened 2D array
 inline constexpr size_t idx2(const size_t i, const size_t j, const size_t n) noexcept {
     return j * n + i;

+            distance = candidate;
+            pathValue = pivotTile * TILE_SIZE + k;
+        }
+    }
+
+    dist[element] = distance;
+    path[element] = pathValue;
+}
+
+float floydWarshallCuda(std::vector<unsigned int>& dist,
+                        std::vector<unsigned int>& path,
+                        const size_t numNodes) {
+    if (numNodes == 0) {
+        return 0.0F;
+    }
+
+    const size_t paddedNodes = ((numNodes + TILE_SIZE - 1) / TILE_SIZE) * TILE_SIZE;
+    if (paddedNodes > std::numeric_limits<unsigned int>::max()) {
+        std::fprintf(stderr, "CUDA Floyd-Warshall supports at most %u nodes\\n",
+                     std::numeric_limits<unsigned int>::max());
+        std::exit(EXIT_FAILURE);
+    }
+
+    const size_t paddedElements = paddedNodes * paddedNodes;
+    std::vector<unsigned int> paddedDist(paddedElements, INF);
+    std::vector<unsigned int> paddedPath(paddedElements, 0);
+    for (size_t destination = 0; destination < numNodes; ++destination) {
+        std::copy_n(dist.data() + destination * numNodes, numNodes,
+                    paddedDist.data() + destination * paddedNodes);
+        std::copy_n(path.data() + destination * numNodes, numNodes,
+                    paddedPath.data() + destination * paddedNodes);
+    }
+
+    unsigned int* deviceDist = nullptr;
+    unsigned int* devicePath = nullptr;
+    const size_t bytes = paddedElements * sizeof(unsigned int);
+    CUDA_CHECK(cudaMalloc(&deviceDist, bytes));
+    CUDA_CHECK(cudaMalloc(&devicePath, bytes));
+    CUDA_CHECK(cudaMemcpy(deviceDist, paddedDist.data(), bytes, cudaMemcpyHostToDevice));
+    CUDA_CHECK(cudaMemcpy(devicePath, paddedPath.data(), bytes, cudaMemcpyHostToDevice));
+
+    cudaEvent_t start{};
+    cudaEvent_t stop{};
+    CUDA_CHECK(cudaEventCreate(&start));
+    CUDA_CHECK(cudaEventCreate(&stop));
+
+    const unsigned int tileCount = static_cast<unsigned int>(paddedNodes / TILE_SIZE);
+    const dim3 threads(TILE_SIZE, TILE_SIZE);
+    CUDA_CHECK(cudaEventRecord(start));
+    for (unsigned int pivotTile = 0; pivotTile < tileCount; ++pivotTile) {
+        floydPivotKernel<<<1, threads>>>(deviceDist, devicePath, paddedNodes, pivotTile);
+        CUDA_CHECK(cudaPeekAtLastError());
+
+        if (tileCount > 1) {
+            floydPivotRowColumnKernel<<<dim3(tileCount - 1, 2), threads>>>(
+                deviceDist, devicePath, paddedNodes, pivotTile);
+            CUDA_CHECK(cudaPeekAtLastError());
+            floydRemainingKernel<<<dim3(tileCount - 1, tileCount - 1), threads>>>(
+                deviceDist, devicePath, paddedNodes, pivotTile);
+            CUDA_CHECK(cudaPeekAtLastError());
+        }
+    }
+    CUDA_CHECK(cudaEventRecord(stop));
+    CUDA_CHECK(cudaEventSynchronize(stop));
+
+    float elapsedMilliseconds = 0.0F;
+    CUDA_CHECK(cudaEventElapsedTime(&elapsedMilliseconds, start, stop));
+
+    CUDA_CHECK(cudaMemcpy2D(dist.data(), numNodes * sizeof(unsigned int),
+                            deviceDist, paddedNodes * sizeof(unsigned int),
+                            numNodes * sizeof(unsigned int), numNodes,
+                            cudaMemcpyDeviceToHost));
+    CUDA_CHECK(cudaMemcpy2D(path.data(), numNodes * sizeof(unsigned int),
+                            devicePath, paddedNodes * sizeof(unsigned int),
+                            numNodes * sizeof(unsigned int), numNodes,
+                            cudaMemcpyDeviceToHost));
+
+    CUDA_CHECK(cudaEventDestroy(start));
+    CUDA_CHECK(cudaEventDestroy(stop));
+    CUDA_CHECK(cudaFree(deviceDist));
+    CUDA_CHECK(cudaFree(devicePath));
+    return elapsedMilliseconds;
+}
+
+}  // namespace

     return j * n + i;

    28	
    29	// Matrices are stored as dist[destination * stride + source].  Threads vary
    30	// source along x so every global-memory matrix load/store is coalesced.
    31	__global__ void floydPivotKernel(unsigned int* __restrict__ dist,
    32	                                 unsigned int* __restrict__ path,
    33	                                 const size_t stride,
    34	                                 const unsigned int pivotTile) {
    35	    __shared__ unsigned int tile[TILE_SIZE][TILE_SIZE + 1];
    36	
    37	    const unsigned int source = pivotTile * TILE_SIZE + threadIdx.x;
    38	    const unsigned int destination = pivotTile * TILE_SIZE + threadIdx.y;
    39	    const size_t element = static_cast<size_t>(destination) * stride + source;
    40	
    41	    tile[threadIdx.y][threadIdx.x] = dist[element];
    42	    unsigned int pathValue = path[element];
    43	    __syncthreads();
    44	
    45	    #pragma unroll
    46	    for (unsigned int k = 0; k < TILE_SIZE; ++k) {
    47	        const unsigned int candidate = tile[k][threadIdx.x] + tile[threadIdx.y][k];
    48	        if (candidate < tile[threadIdx.y][threadIdx.x]) {
    49	            tile[threadIdx.y][threadIdx.x] = candidate;
    50	            pathValue = pivotTile * TILE_SIZE + k;
    51	        }
    52	        __syncthreads();
    53	    }
    54	
    55	    dist[element] = tile[threadIdx.y][threadIdx.x];
    56	    path[element] = pathValue;
    57	}
    58	
    59	// Updates both the pivot column and pivot row.  The second grid dimension
    60	// selects D(other, pivot) or D(pivot, other), respectively.
    61	__global__ void floydPivotRowColumnKernel(unsigned int* __restrict__ dist,
    62	                                          unsigned int* __restrict__ path,
    63	                                          const size_t stride,
    64	                                          const unsigned int pivotTile) {
    65	    __shared__ unsigned int pivot[TILE_SIZE][TILE_SIZE + 1];
    66	    __shared__ unsigned int current[TILE_SIZE][TILE_SIZE + 1];
    67	
    68	    unsigned int otherTile = blockIdx.x;
    69	    if (otherTile >= pivotTile) {
    70	        ++otherTile;
    71	    }
    72	    const bool pivotRow = blockIdx.y != 0;
    73	    const unsigned int sourceTile = pivotRow ? pivotTile : otherTile;
    74	    const unsigned int destinationTile = pivotRow ? otherTile : pivotTile;
    75	    const unsigned int source = sourceTile * TILE_SIZE + threadIdx.x;
    76	    const unsigned int destination = destinationTile * TILE_SIZE + threadIdx.y;
    77	    const unsigned int pivotSource = pivotTile * TILE_SIZE + threadIdx.x;
    78	    const unsigned int pivotDestination = pivotTile * TILE_SIZE + threadIdx.y;
    79	    const size_t element = static_cast<size_t>(destination) * stride + source;
    80	    const size_t pivotElement = static_cast<size_t>(pivotDestination) * stride + pivotSource;
    81	
    82	    current[threadIdx.y][threadIdx.x] = dist[element];
    83	    pivot[threadIdx.y][threadIdx.x] = dist[pivotElement];
    84	    unsigned int pathValue = path[element];
    85	    __syncthreads();
    86	
    87	    #pragma unroll
    88	    for (unsigned int k = 0; k < TILE_SIZE; ++k) {
    89	        const unsigned int left = pivotRow ? pivot[k][threadIdx.x]
    90	                                           : current[k][threadIdx.x];
    91	        const unsigned int right = pivotRow ? current[threadIdx.y][k]
    92	                                            : pivot[threadIdx.y][k];
    93	        const unsigned int candidate = left + right;
    94	        if (candidate < current[threadIdx.y][threadIdx.x]) {
    95	            current[threadIdx.y][threadIdx.x] = candidate;
    96	            pathValue = pivotTile * TILE_SIZE + k;
    97	        }
    98	        __syncthreads();
    99	    }
   100	
   101	    dist[element] = current[threadIdx.y][threadIdx.x];
   102	    path[element] = pathValue;
   103	}
   104	
   105	// The remaining tiles are independent once the pivot row and column have
   106	// been closed.  This phase exposes almost all of the benchmark's parallelism.
   107	__global__ void floydRemainingKernel(unsigned int* __restrict__ dist,
   108	                                     unsigned int* __restrict__ path,
   109	                                     const size_t stride,
   110	                                     const unsigned int pivotTile) {
   111	    __shared__ unsigned int left[TILE_SIZE][TILE_SIZE + 1];
   112	    __shared__ unsigned int right[TILE_SIZE][TILE_SIZE + 1];
   113	
   114	    unsigned int sourceTile = blockIdx.x;
   115	    unsigned int destinationTile = blockIdx.y;
   116	    if (sourceTile >= pivotTile) {
   117	        ++sourceTile;
   118	    }
   119	    if (destinationTile >= pivotTile) {
   120	        ++destinationTile;
   121	    }
   122	
   123	    const unsigned int source = sourceTile * TILE_SIZE + threadIdx.x;
   124	    const unsigned int destination = destinationTile * TILE_SIZE + threadIdx.y;
   125	    const unsigned int pivotSource = pivotTile * TILE_SIZE + threadIdx.x;
   126	    const unsigned int pivotDestination = pivotTile * TILE_SIZE + threadIdx.y;
   127	    const size_t element = static_cast<size_t>(destination) * stride + source;
   128	    const size_t leftElement = static_cast<size_t>(pivotDestination) * stride + source;
   129	    const size_t rightElement = static_cast<size_t>(destination) * stride + pivotSource;
   130	
   131	    left[threadIdx.y][threadIdx.x] = dist[leftElement];
   132	    right[threadIdx.y][threadIdx.x] = dist[rightElement];
   133	    unsigned int distance = dist[element];
   134	    unsigned int pathValue = path[element];
   135	    __syncthreads();
   136	
   137	    #pragma unroll
   138	    for (unsigned int k = 0; k < TILE_SIZE; ++k) {
   139	        const unsigned int candidate = left[k][threadIdx.x] + right[threadIdx.y][k];
   140	        if (candidate < distance) {
   141	            distance = candidate;
   142	            pathValue = pivotTile * TILE_SIZE + k;
   143	        }
   144	    }
   145	
   146	    dist[element] = distance;
   147	    path[element] = pathValue;
   148	}

   231	
   232	void initializeDistanceMatrix(std::vector<unsigned int>& dist, const size_t numNodes, 
   233	                              const unsigned int rangeMin, const unsigned int rangeMax) {
   234	    unsigned int seed = 42;
   235	    const double range = static_cast<double>(rangeMax - rangeMin) + 1.0;
   236	    
   237	    for (size_t i = 0; i < numNodes * numNodes; ++i) {
   238	        dist[i] = rangeMin + (unsigned int)(range * rand_r(&seed) / (double)RAND_MAX);
   239	    }
   240	    
   241	    // Set diagonal to zero (distance from node to itself is 0)
   242	    for (size_t i = 0; i < numNodes; ++i) {
   243	        dist[idx2(i, i, numNodes)] = 0;
   244	    }
   245	}
   246	
   247	void initializePathMatrix(std::vector<unsigned int>& path, const size_t numNodes) {
   248	    for (size_t j = 0; j < numNodes; ++j) {
   249	        for (size_t i = 0; i < numNodes; ++i) {
   250	            path[idx2(i, j, numNodes)] = j;
   251	            path[idx2(j, i, numNodes)] = i;
   252	        }
   253	        path[idx2(j, j, numNodes)] = j;
   254	    }
   255	}
   256	
   257	bool validateResult(const std::vector<unsigned int>& dist, const size_t numNodes) {
   258	    // Basic sanity checks
   259	    
   260	    // 1. Diagonal should be zero
   261	    for (size_t i = 0; i < numNodes; ++i) {
   262	        if (dist[idx2(i, i, numNodes)] != 0) {
   263	            printf("Validation failed: diagonal element [%zu,%zu] is not zero\n", i, i);
   264	            return false;
   265	        }
   266	    }
   267	    
   268	    // 2. Triangle inequality: dist[i][k] + dist[k][j] >= dist[i][j]
   269	    // Check a sample of paths to avoid O(n^3) validation time
   270	    for (size_t i = 0; i < std::min(numNodes, static_cast<size_t>(10)); ++i) {

+namespace {

+// Matrices are stored as dist[destination * stride + source].  Threads vary
+// source along x so every global-memory matrix load/store is coalesced.
+__global__ void floydPivotKernel(unsigned int* __restrict__ dist,
+                                 unsigned int* __restrict__ path,
+                                 const size_t stride,
+                                 const unsigned int pivotTile) {
+    __shared__ unsigned int tile[TILE_SIZE][TILE_SIZE + 1];
+
+    const unsigned int source = pivotTile * TILE_SIZE + threadIdx.x;
+    const unsigned int destination = pivotTile * TILE_SIZE + threadIdx.y;
+    const size_t element = static_cast<size_t>(destination) * stride + source;
+
+    tile[threadIdx.y][threadIdx.x] = dist[element];
+    unsigned int pathValue = path[element];
+    __syncthreads();
+
+    #pragma unroll
+    for (unsigned int k = 0; k < TILE_SIZE; ++k) {
+        const unsigned int candidate = tile[k][threadIdx.x] + tile[threadIdx.y][k];
+        if (candidate < tile[threadIdx.y][threadIdx.x]) {
+            tile[threadIdx.y][threadIdx.x] = candidate;
+            pathValue = pivotTile * TILE_SIZE + k;
+        }
+        __syncthreads();
+    }
+
+    dist[element] = tile[threadIdx.y][threadIdx.x];
+    path[element] = pathValue;
+}
+
+// Updates both the pivot column and pivot row.  The second grid dimension
+// selects D(other, pivot) or D(pivot, other), respectively.
+__global__ void floydPivotRowColumnKernel(unsigned int* __restrict__ dist,
+                                          unsigned int* __restrict__ path,
+                                          const size_t stride,
+                                          const unsigned int pivotTile) {
+    __shared__ unsigned int pivot[TILE_SIZE][TILE_SIZE + 1];
+    __shared__ unsigned int current[TILE_SIZE][TILE_SIZE + 1];
 
+    unsigned int otherTile = blockIdx.x;
+    if (otherTile >= pivotTile) {
+        ++otherTile;
+    }
+    const bool pivotRow = blockIdx.y != 0;
+    const unsigned int sourceTile = pivotRow ? pivotTile : otherTile;
+    const unsigned int destinationTile = pivotRow ? otherTile : pivotTile;
+    const unsigned int source = sourceTile * TILE_SIZE + threadIdx.x;
+    const unsigned int destination = destinationTile * TILE_SIZE + threadIdx.y;
+    const unsigned int pivotSource = pivotTile * TILE_SIZE + threadIdx.x;
+    const unsigned int pivotDestination = pivotTile * TILE_SIZE + threadIdx.y;
+    const size_t element = static_cast<size_t>(destination) * stride + source;
+    const size_t pivotElement = static_cast<size_t>(pivotDestination) * stride + pivotSource;
+
+    current[threadIdx.y][threadIdx.x] = dist[element];
+    pivot[threadIdx.y][threadIdx.x] = dist[pivotElement];
+    unsigned int pathValue = path[element];
+    __syncthreads();
+
+    #pragma unroll
+    for (unsigned int k = 0; k < TILE_SIZE; ++k) {
+        const unsigned int left = pivotRow ? pivot[k][threadIdx.x]
+                                           : current[k][threadIdx.x];
+        const unsigned int right = pivotRow ? current[threadIdx.y][k]
+                                            : pivot[threadIdx.y][k];
+        const unsigned int candidate = left + right;
+        if (candidate < current[threadIdx.y][threadIdx.x]) {
+            current[threadIdx.y][threadIdx.x] = candidate;
+            pathValue = pivotTile * TILE_SIZE + k;
+        }
+        __syncthreads();
+    }
+
+    dist[element] = current[threadIdx.y][threadIdx.x];
+    path[element] = pathValue;
+}
+
+// The remaining tiles are independent once the pivot row and column have
+// been closed.  This phase exposes almost all of the benchmark's parallelism.
+__global__ void floydRemainingKernel(unsigned int* __restrict__ dist,
+                                     unsigned int* __restrict__ path,
+                                     const size_t stride,
+                                     const unsigned int pivotTile) {
+    __shared__ unsigned int left[TILE_SIZE][TILE_SIZE + 1];
+    __shared__ unsigned int right[TILE_SIZE][TILE_SIZE + 1];
+
+    unsigned int sourceTile = blockIdx.x;
+    unsigned int destinationTile = blockIdx.y;
+    if (sourceTile >= pivotTile) {
+        ++sourceTile;
+    }
+    if (destinationTile >= pivotTile) {
+        ++destinationTile;
+    }
+
+    const unsigned int source = sourceTile * TILE_SIZE + threadIdx.x;
+    const unsigned int destination = destinationTile * TILE_SIZE + threadIdx.y;
+    const unsigned int pivotSource = pivotTile * TILE_SIZE + threadIdx.x;
+    const unsigned int pivotDestination = pivotTile * TILE_SIZE + threadIdx.y;
+    const size_t element = static_cast<size_t>(destination) * stride + source;
+    const size_t leftElement = static_cast<size_t>(pivotDestination) * stride + source;
+    const size_t rightElement = static_cast<size_t>(destination) * stride + pivotSource;
+
+    left[threadIdx.y][threadIdx.x] = dist[leftElement];
+    right[threadIdx.y][threadIdx.x] = dist[rightElement];
+    unsigned int distance = dist[element];
+    unsigned int pathValue = path[element];
+    __syncthreads();
+
+    #pragma unroll
+    for (unsigned int k = 0; k < TILE_SIZE; ++k) {
+        const unsigned int candidate = left[k][threadIdx.x] + right[threadIdx.y][k];
+        if (candidate < distance) {
+            distance = candidate;
+            pathValue = pivotTile * TILE_SIZE + k;
+        }
+    }
+
+    dist[element] = distance;
+    path[element] = pathValue;
+}
+
+float floydWarshallCuda(std::vector<unsigned int>& dist,
+                        std::vector<unsigned int>& path,
+                        const size_t numNodes) {
+    if (numNodes == 0) {
+        return 0.0F;
+    }
+
+    const size_t paddedNodes = ((numNodes + TILE_SIZE - 1) / TILE_SIZE) * TILE_SIZE;
+    if (paddedNodes > std::numeric_limits<unsigned int>::max()) {
+        std::fprintf(stderr, "CUDA Floyd-Warshall supports at most %u nodes\\n",
+                     std::numeric_limits<unsigned int>::max());
+        std::exit(EXIT_FAILURE);
+    }
+
+    const size_t paddedElements = paddedNodes * paddedNodes;
+    std::vector<unsigned int> paddedDist(paddedElements, INF);
+    std::vector<unsigned int> paddedPath(paddedElements, 0);
+    for (size_t destination = 0; destination < numNodes; ++destination) {
+        std::copy_n(dist.data() + destination * numNodes, numNodes,
+                    paddedDist.data() + destination * paddedNodes);
+        std::copy_n(path.data() + destination * numNodes, numNodes,
+                    paddedPath.data() + destination * paddedNodes);
+    }
+
+    unsigned int* deviceDist = nullptr;
+    unsigned int* devicePath = nullptr;
+    const size_t bytes = paddedElements * sizeof(unsigned int);
+    CUDA_CHECK(cudaMalloc(&deviceDist, bytes));
+    CUDA_CHECK(cudaMalloc(&devicePath, bytes));
+    CUDA_CHECK(cudaMemcpy(deviceDist, paddedDist.data(), bytes, cudaMemcpyHostToDevice));
+    CUDA_CHECK(cudaMemcpy(devicePath, paddedPath.data(), bytes, cudaMemcpyHostToDevice));
+
+    cudaEvent_t start{};
+    cudaEvent_t stop{};
+    CUDA_CHECK(cudaEventCreate(&start));
+    CUDA_CHECK(cudaEventCreate(&stop));
+
+    const unsigned int tileCount = static_cast<unsigned int>(paddedNodes / TILE_SIZE);
+    const dim3 threads(TILE_SIZE, TILE_SIZE);
+    CUDA_CHECK(cudaEventRecord(start));
+    for (unsigned int pivotTile = 0; pivotTile < tileCount; ++pivotTile) {
+        floydPivotKernel<<<1, threads>>>(deviceDist, devicePath, paddedNodes, pivotTile);
+        CUDA_CHECK(cudaPeekAtLastError());
+
+        if (tileCount > 1) {
+            floydPivotRowColumnKernel<<<dim3(tileCount - 1, 2), threads>>>(
+                deviceDist, devicePath, paddedNodes, pivotTile);
+            CUDA_CHECK(cudaPeekAtLastError());
+            floydRemainingKernel<<<dim3(tileCount - 1, tileCount - 1), threads>>>(
+                deviceDist, devicePath, paddedNodes, pivotTile);
+            CUDA_CHECK(cudaPeekAtLastError());
+        }
+    }
+    CUDA_CHECK(cudaEventRecord(stop));
+    CUDA_CHECK(cudaEventSynchronize(stop));
+
+    float elapsedMilliseconds = 0.0F;
+    CUDA_CHECK(cudaEventElapsedTime(&elapsedMilliseconds, start, stop));
+
+    CUDA_CHECK(cudaMemcpy2D(dist.data(), numNodes * sizeof(unsigned int),
+                            deviceDist, paddedNodes * sizeof(unsigned int),
+                            numNodes * sizeof(unsigned int), numNodes,
+                            cudaMemcpyDeviceToHost));
+    CUDA_CHECK(cudaMemcpy2D(path.data(), numNodes * sizeof(unsigned int),
+                            devicePath, paddedNodes * sizeof(unsigned int),
+                            numNodes * sizeof(unsigned int), numNodes,
+                            cudaMemcpyDeviceToHost));
+
+    CUDA_CHECK(cudaEventDestroy(start));
+    CUDA_CHECK(cudaEventDestroy(stop));
+    CUDA_CHECK(cudaFree(deviceDist));
+    CUDA_CHECK(cudaFree(devicePath));
+    return elapsedMilliseconds;
+}
+
+}  // namespace
+

     return j * n + i;

 constexpr unsigned int INF = 1000000000;
 constexpr unsigned int MAX_DISTANCE = 200;
+constexpr unsigned int TILE_SIZE = 32;
+constexpr unsigned int THREAD_ROWS = 8;
+constexpr unsigned int OUTPUTS_PER_THREAD = TILE_SIZE / THREAD_ROWS;
+
+#define CUDA_CHECK(call)                                                        \
+    do {                                                                        \
+        const cudaError_t error_ = (call);                                     \
+        if (error_ != cudaSuccess) {                                           \

+namespace {

+// Matrices are stored as dist[destination * stride + source].  Threads vary
+// source along x so every global-memory matrix load/store is coalesced.
+__global__ void floydPivotKernel(unsigned int* __restrict__ dist,
+                                 unsigned int* __restrict__ path,
+                                 const size_t stride,
+                                 const unsigned int pivotTile) {
+    __shared__ unsigned int tile[TILE_SIZE][TILE_SIZE + 1];
+
+    const unsigned int source = pivotTile * TILE_SIZE + threadIdx.x;
+    const unsigned int destinationBase = pivotTile * TILE_SIZE + threadIdx.y;
+    unsigned int pathValue[OUTPUTS_PER_THREAD];
+
+    #pragma unroll
+    for (unsigned int output = 0; output < OUTPUTS_PER_THREAD; ++output) {
+        const unsigned int destination = destinationBase + output * THREAD_ROWS;
+        const size_t element = static_cast<size_t>(destination) * stride + source;
+        tile[threadIdx.y + output * THREAD_ROWS][threadIdx.x] = dist[element];
+        pathValue[output] = path[element];
+    }
+    __syncthreads();
+
+    #pragma unroll
+    for (unsigned int k = 0; k < TILE_SIZE; ++k) {
+        const unsigned int left = tile[k][threadIdx.x];
+        #pragma unroll
+        for (unsigned int output = 0; output < OUTPUTS_PER_THREAD; ++output) {
+            const unsigned int row = threadIdx.y + output * THREAD_ROWS;
+            const unsigned int candidate = left + tile[row][k];
+            if (candidate < tile[row][threadIdx.x]) {
+                tile[row][threadIdx.x] = candidate;
+                pathValue[output] = pivotTile * TILE_SIZE + k;
+            }
+        }
+        __syncthreads();
+    }
+
+    #pragma unroll
+    for (unsigned int output = 0; output < OUTPUTS_PER_THREAD; ++output) {
+        const unsigned int destination = destinationBase + output * THREAD_ROWS;
+        const size_t element = static_cast<size_t>(destination) * stride + source;
+        dist[element] = tile[threadIdx.y + output * THREAD_ROWS][threadIdx.x];
+        path[element] = pathValue[output];
+    }
+}
+
+// Updates both the pivot column and pivot row.  The second grid dimension
+// selects D(other, pivot) or D(pivot, other), respectively.
+__global__ void floydPivotRowColumnKernel(unsigned int* __restrict__ dist,
+                                          unsigned int* __restrict__ path,
+                                          const size_t stride,
+                                          const unsigned int pivotTile) {
+    __shared__ unsigned int pivot[TILE_SIZE][TILE_SIZE + 1];
+    __shared__ unsigned int current[TILE_SIZE][TILE_SIZE + 1];
+
+    unsigned int otherTile = blockIdx.x;
+    if (otherTile >= pivotTile) {
+        ++otherTile;
+    }
+    const bool pivotRow = blockIdx.y != 0;
+    const unsigned int sourceTile = pivotRow ? pivotTile : otherTile;
+    const unsigned int destinationTile = pivotRow ? otherTile : pivotTile;
+    const unsigned int source = sourceTile * TILE_SIZE + threadIdx.x;
+    const unsigned int destinationBase = destinationTile * TILE_SIZE + threadIdx.y;
+    const unsigned int pivotSource = pivotTile * TILE_SIZE + threadIdx.x;
+    const unsigned int pivotDestinationBase = pivotTile * TILE_SIZE + threadIdx.y;
+    unsigned int pathValue[OUTPUTS_PER_THREAD];
+
+    #pragma unroll
+    for (unsigned int output = 0; output < OUTPUTS_PER_THREAD; ++output) {
+        const unsigned int row = threadIdx.y + output * THREAD_ROWS;
+        const unsigned int destination = destinationBase + output * THREAD_ROWS;
+        const unsigned int pivotDestination = pivotDestinationBase + output * THREAD_ROWS;
+        const size_t element = static_cast<size_t>(destination) * stride + source;
+        const size_t pivotElement = static_cast<size_t>(pivotDestination) * stride + pivotSource;
+        current[row][threadIdx.x] = dist[element];
+        pivot[row][threadIdx.x] = dist[pivotElement];
+        pathValue[output] = path[element];
+    }
+    __syncthreads();
+
+    #pragma unroll
+    for (unsigned int k = 0; k < TILE_SIZE; ++k) {
+        const unsigned int left = pivotRow ? pivot[k][threadIdx.x]
+                                           : current[k][threadIdx.x];
+        #pragma unroll
+        for (unsigned int output = 0; output < OUTPUTS_PER_THREAD; ++output) {
+            const unsigned int row = threadIdx.y + output * THREAD_ROWS;
+            const unsigned int right = pivotRow ? current[row][k] : pivot[row][k];
+            const unsigned int candidate = left + right;
+            if (candidate < current[row][threadIdx.x]) {
+                current[row][threadIdx.x] = candidate;
+                pathValue[output] = pivotTile * TILE_SIZE + k;
+            }
+        }
+        __syncthreads();
+    }
+
+    #pragma unroll
+    for (unsigned int output = 0; output < OUTPUTS_PER_THREAD; ++output) {
+        const unsigned int destination = destinationBase + output * THREAD_ROWS;
+        const size_t element = static_cast<size_t>(destination) * stride + source;
+        dist[element] = current[threadIdx.y + output * THREAD_ROWS][threadIdx.x];
+        path[element] = pathValue[output];
+    }
+}
+
+// The remaining tiles are independent once the pivot row and column have
+// been closed.  This phase exposes almost all of the benchmark's parallelism.
+__global__ void floydRemainingKernel(unsigned int* __restrict__ dist,
+                                     unsigned int* __restrict__ path,
+                                     const size_t stride,
+                                     const unsigned int pivotTile) {
+    __shared__ unsigned int left[TILE_SIZE][TILE_SIZE + 1];
+    __shared__ unsigned int right[TILE_SIZE][TILE_SIZE + 1];
+
+    unsigned int sourceTile = blockIdx.x;
+    unsigned int destinationTile = blockIdx.y;
+    if (sourceTile >= pivotTile) {
+        ++sourceTile;
+    }
+    if (destinationTile >= pivotTile) {
+        ++destinationTile;
+    }
 
+    const unsigned int source = sourceTile * TILE_SIZE + threadIdx.x;
+    const unsigned int destinationBase = destinationTile * TILE_SIZE + threadIdx.y;
+    const unsigned int pivotSource = pivotTile * TILE_SIZE + threadIdx.x;
+    const unsigned int pivotDestinationBase = pivotTile * TILE_SIZE + threadIdx.y;
+    unsigned int distance[OUTPUTS_PER_THREAD];
+    unsigned int pathValue[OUTPUTS_PER_THREAD];
+
+    #pragma unroll
+    for (unsigned int output = 0; output < OUTPUTS_PER_THREAD; ++output) {
+        const unsigned int row = threadIdx.y + output * THREAD_ROWS;
+        const unsigned int destination = destinationBase + output * THREAD_ROWS;
+        const unsigned int pivotDestination = pivotDestinationBase + output * THREAD_ROWS;
+        const size_t element = static_cast<size_t>(destination) * stride + source;
+        const size_t leftElement = static_cast<size_t>(pivotDestination) * stride + source;
+        const size_t rightElement = static_cast<size_t>(destination) * stride + pivotSource;
+        left[row][threadIdx.x] = dist[leftElement];
+        right[row][threadIdx.x] = dist[rightElement];
+        distance[output] = dist[element];
+        pathValue[output] = path[element];
+    }
+    __syncthreads();
+
+    #pragma unroll
+    for (unsigned int k = 0; k < TILE_SIZE; ++k) {
+        const unsigned int leftDistance = left[k][threadIdx.x];
+        #pragma unroll
+        for (unsigned int output = 0; output < OUTPUTS_PER_THREAD; ++output) {
+            const unsigned int row = threadIdx.y + output * THREAD_ROWS;
+            const unsigned int candidate = leftDistance + right[row][k];
+            if (candidate < distance[output]) {
+                distance[output] = candidate;
+                pathValue[output] = pivotTile * TILE_SIZE + k;
+            }
+        }
+    }
+
+    #pragma unroll
+    for (unsigned int output = 0; output < OUTPUTS_PER_THREAD; ++output) {
+        const unsigned int destination = destinationBase + output * THREAD_ROWS;
+        const size_t element = static_cast<size_t>(destination) * stride + source;
+        dist[element] = distance[output];
+        path[element] = pathValue[output];
+    }
+}
+
+float floydWarshallCuda(std::vector<unsigned int>& dist,
+                        std::vector<unsigned int>& path,
+                        const size_t numNodes) {
+    if (numNodes == 0) {
+        return 0.0F;
+    }
+
+    const size_t paddedNodes = ((numNodes + TILE_SIZE - 1) / TILE_SIZE) * TILE_SIZE;
+    if (paddedNodes > std::numeric_limits<unsigned int>::max()) {
+        std::fprintf(stderr, "CUDA Floyd-Warshall supports at most %u nodes\\n",
+                     std::numeric_limits<unsigned int>::max());
+        std::exit(EXIT_FAILURE);
+    }
+
+    const size_t paddedElements = paddedNodes * paddedNodes;
+    std::vector<unsigned int> paddedDist(paddedElements, INF);
+    std::vector<unsigned int> paddedPath(paddedElements, 0);
+    for (size_t destination = 0; destination < numNodes; ++destination) {
+        std::copy_n(dist.data() + destination * numNodes, numNodes,
+                    paddedDist.data() + destination * paddedNodes);
+        std::copy_n(path.data() + destination * numNodes, numNodes,
+                    paddedPath.data() + destination * paddedNodes);
+    }
+
+    unsigned int* deviceDist = nullptr;
+    unsigned int* devicePath = nullptr;
+    const size_t bytes = paddedElements * sizeof(unsigned int);
+    CUDA_CHECK(cudaMalloc(&deviceDist, bytes));
+    CUDA_CHECK(cudaMalloc(&devicePath, bytes));
+    CUDA_CHECK(cudaMemcpy(deviceDist, paddedDist.data(), bytes, cudaMemcpyHostToDevice));
+    CUDA_CHECK(cudaMemcpy(devicePath, paddedPath.data(), bytes, cudaMemcpyHostToDevice));
+
+    cudaEvent_t start{};
+    cudaEvent_t stop{};
+    CUDA_CHECK(cudaEventCreate(&start));
+    CUDA_CHECK(cudaEventCreate(&stop));
+
+    const unsigned int tileCount = static_cast<unsigned int>(paddedNodes / TILE_SIZE);
+    const dim3 threads(TILE_SIZE, THREAD_ROWS);
+    CUDA_CHECK(cudaEventRecord(start));
+    for (unsigned int pivotTile = 0; pivotTile < tileCount; ++pivotTile) {
+        floydPivotKernel<<<1, threads>>>(deviceDist, devicePath, paddedNodes, pivotTile);
+        CUDA_CHECK(cudaPeekAtLastError());
+
+        if (tileCount > 1) {
+            floydPivotRowColumnKernel<<<dim3(tileCount - 1, 2), threads>>>(
+                deviceDist, devicePath, paddedNodes, pivotTile);
+            CUDA_CHECK(cudaPeekAtLastError());
+            floydRemainingKernel<<<dim3(tileCount - 1, tileCount - 1), threads>>>(
+                deviceDist, devicePath, paddedNodes, pivotTile);
+            CUDA_CHECK(cudaPeekAtLastError());
+        }
+    }
+    CUDA_CHECK(cudaEventRecord(stop));
+    CUDA_CHECK(cudaEventSynchronize(stop));
+
+    float elapsedMilliseconds = 0.0F;
+    CUDA_CHECK(cudaEventElapsedTime(&elapsedMilliseconds, start, stop));
+
+    CUDA_CHECK(cudaMemcpy2D(dist.data(), numNodes * sizeof(unsigned int),
+                            deviceDist, paddedNodes * sizeof(unsigned int),
+                            numNodes * sizeof(unsigned int), numNodes,
+                            cudaMemcpyDeviceToHost));
+    CUDA_CHECK(cudaMemcpy2D(path.data(), numNodes * sizeof(unsigned int),
+                            devicePath, paddedNodes * sizeof(unsigned int),
+                            numNodes * sizeof(unsigned int), numNodes,
+                            cudaMemcpyDeviceToHost));
+
+    CUDA_CHECK(cudaEventDestroy(start));
+    CUDA_CHECK(cudaEventDestroy(stop));
+    CUDA_CHECK(cudaFree(deviceDist));
+    CUDA_CHECK(cudaFree(devicePath));
+    return elapsedMilliseconds;
+}

 #include <algorithm>

 #include <cmath>

 #include "../common/results_output.hpp"
 
 constexpr unsigned int INF = 1000000000;
 constexpr unsigned int MAX_DISTANCE = 200;
+constexpr unsigned int TILE_SIZE = 32;
+constexpr unsigned int THREAD_ROWS = 8;
+constexpr unsigned int OUTPUTS_PER_THREAD = TILE_SIZE / THREAD_ROWS;
+
+#define CUDA_CHECK(call)                                                        \
+    do {                                                                        \
+        const cudaError_t error_ = (call);                                     \
+        if (error_ != cudaSuccess) {                                           \
+            std::fprintf(stderr, "CUDA error at %s:%d: %s\\n", __FILE__,     \
+                         __LINE__, cudaGetErrorString(error_));                \
+            std::exit(EXIT_FAILURE);                                           \
+        }                                                                       \
+    } while (false)

+namespace {
+
+// Matrices are stored as dist[destination * stride + source].  Threads vary
+// source along x so every global-memory matrix load/store is coalesced.
+__global__ void floydPivotKernel(unsigned int* __restrict__ dist,
+                                 unsigned int* __restrict__ path,
+                                 const size_t stride,
+                                 const unsigned int pivotTile) {
+    __shared__ unsigned int tile[TILE_SIZE][TILE_SIZE + 1];
+
+    const unsigned int source = pivotTile * TILE_SIZE + threadIdx.x;
+    const unsigned int destinationBase = pivotTile * TILE_SIZE + threadIdx.y;
+    unsigned int pathValue[OUTPUTS_PER_THREAD];
+
+    #pragma unroll
+    for (unsigned int output = 0; output < OUTPUTS_PER_THREAD; ++output) {
+        const unsigned int destination = destinationBase + output * THREAD_ROWS;
+        const size_t element = static_cast<size_t>(destination) * stride + source;
+        tile[threadIdx.y + output * THREAD_ROWS][threadIdx.x] = dist[element];
+        pathValue[output] = path[element];
+    }
+    __syncthreads();
+
+    #pragma unroll
+    for (unsigned int k = 0; k < TILE_SIZE; ++k) {
+        const unsigned int left = tile[k][threadIdx.x];
+        #pragma unroll
+        for (unsigned int output = 0; output < OUTPUTS_PER_THREAD; ++output) {
+            const unsigned int row = threadIdx.y + output * THREAD_ROWS;
+            const unsigned int candidate = left + tile[row][k];
+            if (candidate < tile[row][threadIdx.x]) {
+                tile[row][threadIdx.x] = candidate;
+                pathValue[output] = pivotTile * TILE_SIZE + k;
+            }
+        }
+        __syncthreads();
+    }
+
+    #pragma unroll
+    for (unsigned int output = 0; output < OUTPUTS_PER_THREAD; ++output) {
+        const unsigned int destination = destinationBase + output * THREAD_ROWS;
+        const size_t element = static_cast<size_t>(destination) * stride + source;
+        dist[element] = tile[threadIdx.y + output * THREAD_ROWS][threadIdx.x];
+        path[element] = pathValue[output];
+    }
+}
+
+// Updates both the pivot column and pivot row.  The second grid dimension
+// selects D(other, pivot) or D(pivot, other), respectively.
+__global__ void floydPivotRowColumnKernel(unsigned int* __restrict__ dist,
+                                          unsigned int* __restrict__ path,
+                                          const size_t stride,
+                                          const unsigned int pivotTile) {
+    __shared__ unsigned int pivot[TILE_SIZE][TILE_SIZE + 1];
+    __shared__ unsigned int current[TILE_SIZE][TILE_SIZE + 1];
+
+    unsigned int otherTile = blockIdx.x;
+    if (otherTile >= pivotTile) {
+        ++otherTile;
+    }
+    const bool pivotRow = blockIdx.y != 0;
+    const unsigned int sourceTile = pivotRow ? pivotTile : otherTile;
+    const unsigned int destinationTile = pivotRow ? otherTile : pivotTile;
+    const unsigned int source = sourceTile * TILE_SIZE + threadIdx.x;
+    const unsigned int destinationBase = destinationTile * TILE_SIZE + threadIdx.y;
+    const unsigned int pivotSource = pivotTile * TILE_SIZE + threadIdx.x;
+    const unsigned int pivotDestinationBase = pivotTile * TILE_SIZE + threadIdx.y;
+    unsigned int pathValue[OUTPUTS_PER_THREAD];
+
+    #pragma unroll
+    for (unsigned int output = 0; output < OUTPUTS_PER_THREAD; ++output) {
+        const unsigned int row = threadIdx.y + output * THREAD_ROWS;
+        const unsigned int destination = destinationBase + output * THREAD_ROWS;
+        const unsigned int pivotDestination = pivotDestinationBase + output * THREAD_ROWS;
+        const size_t element = static_cast<size_t>(destination) * stride + source;
+        const size_t pivotElement = static_cast<size_t>(pivotDestination) * stride + pivotSource;
+        current[row][threadIdx.x] = dist[element];
+        pivot[row][threadIdx.x] = dist[pivotElement];
+        pathValue[output] = path[element];
+    }
+    __syncthreads();
+
+    #pragma unroll
+    for (unsigned int k = 0; k < TILE_SIZE; ++k) {
+        const unsigned int left = pivotRow ? pivot[k][threadIdx.x]
+                                           : current[k][threadIdx.x];
+        #pragma unroll
+        for (unsigned int output = 0; output < OUTPUTS_PER_THREAD; ++output) {
+            const unsigned int row = threadIdx.y + output * THREAD_ROWS;
+            const unsigned int right = pivotRow ? current[row][k] : pivot[row][k];
+            const unsigned int candidate = left + right;
+            if (candidate < current[row][threadIdx.x]) {
+                current[row][threadIdx.x] = candidate;
+                pathValue[output] = pivotTile * TILE_SIZE + k;
+            }
+        }
+        __syncthreads();
+    }
+
+    #pragma unroll
+    for (unsigned int output = 0; output < OUTPUTS_PER_THREAD; ++output) {
+        const unsigned int destination = destinationBase + output * THREAD_ROWS;
+        const size_t element = static_cast<size_t>(destination) * stride + source;
+        dist[element] = current[threadIdx.y + output * THREAD_ROWS][threadIdx.x];
+        path[element] = pathValue[output];
+    }
+}
+
+// The remaining tiles are independent once the pivot row and column have
+// been closed.  This phase exposes almost all of the benchmark's parallelism.
+__global__ void floydRemainingKernel(unsigned int* __restrict__ dist,
+                                     unsigned int* __restrict__ path,
+                                     const size_t stride,
+                                     const unsigned int pivotTile) {
+    __shared__ unsigned int left[TILE_SIZE][TILE_SIZE + 1];
+    __shared__ unsigned int right[TILE_SIZE][TILE_SIZE + 1];
+
+    unsigned int sourceTile = blockIdx.x;
+    unsigned int destinationTile = blockIdx.y;
+    if (sourceTile >= pivotTile) {
+        ++sourceTile;
+    }
+    if (destinationTile >= pivotTile) {
+        ++destinationTile;
+    }
 
+    const unsigned int source = sourceTile * TILE_SIZE + threadIdx.x;
+    const unsigned int destinationBase = destinationTile * TILE_SIZE + threadIdx.y;
+    const unsigned int pivotSource = pivotTile * TILE_SIZE + threadIdx.x;
+    const unsigned int pivotDestinationBase = pivotTile * TILE_SIZE + threadIdx.y;
+    unsigned int distance[OUTPUTS_PER_THREAD];
+    unsigned int pathValue[OUTPUTS_PER_THREAD];
+
+    #pragma unroll
+    for (unsigned int output = 0; output < OUTPUTS_PER_THREAD; ++output) {
+        const unsigned int row = threadIdx.y + output * THREAD_ROWS;
+        const unsigned int destination = destinationBase + output * THREAD_ROWS;
+        const unsigned int pivotDestination = pivotDestinationBase + output * THREAD_ROWS;
+        const size_t element = static_cast<size_t>(destination) * stride + source;
+        const size_t leftElement = static_cast<size_t>(pivotDestination) * stride + source;
+        const size_t rightElement = static_cast<size_t>(destination) * stride + pivotSource;
+        left[row][threadIdx.x] = dist[leftElement];
+        right[row][threadIdx.x] = dist[rightElement];
+        distance[output] = dist[element];
+        pathValue[output] = path[element];
+    }
+    __syncthreads();
+
+    #pragma unroll
+    for (unsigned int k = 0; k < TILE_SIZE; ++k) {
+        const unsigned int leftDistance = left[k][threadIdx.x];
+        #pragma unroll
+        for (unsigned int output = 0; output < OUTPUTS_PER_THREAD; ++output) {
+            const unsigned int row = threadIdx.y + output * THREAD_ROWS;
+            const unsigned int candidate = leftDistance + right[row][k];
+            if (candidate < distance[output]) {
+                distance[output] = candidate;
+                pathValue[output] = pivotTile * TILE_SIZE + k;
+            }
+        }
+    }
+
+    #pragma unroll
+    for (unsigned int output = 0; output < OUTPUTS_PER_THREAD; ++output) {
+        const unsigned int destination = destinationBase + output * THREAD_ROWS;
+        const size_t element = static_cast<size_t>(destination) * stride + source;
+        dist[element] = distance[output];
+        path[element] = pathValue[output];
+    }
+}
+
+float floydWarshallCuda(std::vector<unsigned int>& dist,
+                        std::vector<unsigned int>& path,
+                        const size_t numNodes) {
+    if (numNodes == 0) {
+        return 0.0F;
+    }
+
+    const size_t paddedNodes = ((numNodes + TILE_SIZE - 1) / TILE_SIZE) * TILE_SIZE;
+    if (paddedNodes > std::numeric_limits<unsigned int>::max()) {
+        std::fprintf(stderr, "CUDA Floyd-Warshall supports at most %u nodes\\n",
+                     std::numeric_limits<unsigned int>::max());
+        std::exit(EXIT_FAILURE);
+    }
+
+    const size_t paddedElements = paddedNodes * paddedNodes;
+    std::vector<unsigned int> paddedDist(paddedElements, INF);
+    std::vector<unsigned int> paddedPath(paddedElements, 0);
+    for (size_t destination = 0; destination < numNodes; ++destination) {
+        std::copy_n(dist.data() + destination * numNodes, numNodes,
+                    paddedDist.data() + destination * paddedNodes);
+        std::copy_n(path.data() + destination * numNodes, numNodes,
+                    paddedPath.data() + destination * paddedNodes);
+    }
+
+    unsigned int* deviceDist = nullptr;
+    unsigned int* devicePath = nullptr;
+    const size_t bytes = paddedElements * sizeof(unsigned int);
+    CUDA_CHECK(cudaMalloc(&deviceDist, bytes));
+    CUDA_CHECK(cudaMalloc(&devicePath, bytes));
+    CUDA_CHECK(cudaMemcpy(deviceDist, paddedDist.data(), bytes, cudaMemcpyHostToDevice));
+    CUDA_CHECK(cudaMemcpy(devicePath, paddedPath.data(), bytes, cudaMemcpyHostToDevice));
+
+    cudaEvent_t start{};
+    cudaEvent_t stop{};
+    CUDA_CHECK(cudaEventCreate(&start));
+    CUDA_CHECK(cudaEventCreate(&stop));
+
+    const unsigned int tileCount = static_cast<unsigned int>(paddedNodes / TILE_SIZE);
+    const dim3 threads(TILE_SIZE, THREAD_ROWS);
+    CUDA_CHECK(cudaEventRecord(start));
+    for (unsigned int pivotTile = 0; pivotTile < tileCount; ++pivotTile) {
+        floydPivotKernel<<<1, threads>>>(deviceDist, devicePath, paddedNodes, pivotTile);
+        CUDA_CHECK(cudaPeekAtLastError());
+
+        if (tileCount > 1) {
+            floydPivotRowColumnKernel<<<dim3(tileCount - 1, 2), threads>>>(
+                deviceDist, devicePath, paddedNodes, pivotTile);
+            CUDA_CHECK(cudaPeekAtLastError());
+            floydRemainingKernel<<<dim3(tileCount - 1, tileCount - 1), threads>>>(
+                deviceDist, devicePath, paddedNodes, pivotTile);
+            CUDA_CHECK(cudaPeekAtLastError());
+        }
+    }
+    CUDA_CHECK(cudaEventRecord(stop));
+    CUDA_CHECK(cudaEventSynchronize(stop));
+
+    float elapsedMilliseconds = 0.0F;
+    CUDA_CHECK(cudaEventElapsedTime(&elapsedMilliseconds, start, stop));
+
+    CUDA_CHECK(cudaMemcpy2D(dist.data(), numNodes * sizeof(unsigned int),
+                            deviceDist, paddedNodes * sizeof(unsigned int),
+                            numNodes * sizeof(unsigned int), numNodes,
+                            cudaMemcpyDeviceToHost));
+    CUDA_CHECK(cudaMemcpy2D(path.data(), numNodes * sizeof(unsigned int),
+                            devicePath, paddedNodes * sizeof(unsigned int),
+                            numNodes * sizeof(unsigned int), numNodes,
+                            cudaMemcpyDeviceToHost));
+
+    CUDA_CHECK(cudaEventDestroy(start));
+    CUDA_CHECK(cudaEventDestroy(stop));
+    CUDA_CHECK(cudaFree(deviceDist));
+    CUDA_CHECK(cudaFree(devicePath));
+    return elapsedMilliseconds;
+}
+
+}  // namespace
+
 // Index calculation for flattened 2D array
 inline constexpr size_t idx2(const size_t i, const size_t j, const size_t n) noexcept {
     return j * n + i;

+__global__ void floydPivotRowColumnKernel(unsigned int* __restrict__ dist,

+
+    #pragma unroll
+    for (unsigned int output = 0; output < OUTPUTS_PER_THREAD; ++output) {
+        const unsigned int row = threadIdx.y + output * THREAD_ROWS;
+        const unsigned int destination = destinationBase + output * THREAD_ROWS;
+        const unsigned int pivotDestination = pivotDestinationBase + output * THREAD_ROWS;
+        const size_t element = static_cast<size_t>(destination) * stride + source;
+        const size_t pivotElement = static_cast<size_t>(pivotDestination) * stride + pivotSource;
+        current[row][threadIdx.x] = dist[element];
+        pivot[row][threadIdx.x] = dist[pivotElement];
+        pathValue[output] = path[element];
+    }
+    __syncthreads();
+
+    #pragma unroll
+    for (unsigned int k = 0; k < TILE_SIZE; ++k) {
+        const unsigned int left = pivotRow ? pivot[k][threadIdx.x]
+                                           : current[k][threadIdx.x];
+        #pragma unroll
+        for (unsigned int output = 0; output < OUTPUTS_PER_THREAD; ++output) {
+            const unsigned int row = threadIdx.y + output * THREAD_ROWS;
+            const unsigned int right = pivotRow ? current[row][k] : pivot[row][k];
+            const unsigned int candidate = left + right;
+            if (candidate < current[row][threadIdx.x]) {
+                current[row][threadIdx.x] = candidate;
+                pathValue[output] = pivotTile * TILE_SIZE + k;
+            }
+        }
+        __syncthreads();
+    }
+
+    #pragma unroll
+    for (unsigned int output = 0; output < OUTPUTS_PER_THREAD; ++output) {
+        const unsigned int destination = destinationBase + output * THREAD_ROWS;
+        const size_t element = static_cast<size_t>(destination) * stride + source;
+        dist[element] = current[threadIdx.y + output * THREAD_ROWS][threadIdx.x];
+        path[element] = pathValue[output];
+    }
+}
+
+// The remaining tiles are independent once the pivot row and column have
+// been closed.  This phase exposes almost all of the benchmark's parallelism.
+__global__ void floydRemainingKernel(unsigned int* __restrict__ dist,
+                                     unsigned int* __restrict__ path,
+                                     const size_t stride,
+                                     const unsigned int pivotTile) {
+    __shared__ unsigned int left[TILE_SIZE][TILE_SIZE + 1];
+    __shared__ unsigned int right[TILE_SIZE][TILE_SIZE + 1];
+
+    unsigned int sourceTile = blockIdx.x;
+    unsigned int destinationTile = blockIdx.y;
+    if (sourceTile >= pivotTile) {
+        ++sourceTile;
+    }
+    if (destinationTile >= pivotTile) {
+        ++destinationTile;
+    }
 
+    const unsigned int source = sourceTile * TILE_SIZE + threadIdx.x;
+    const unsigned int destinationBase = destinationTile * TILE_SIZE + threadIdx.y;
+    const unsigned int pivotSource = pivotTile * TILE_SIZE + threadIdx.x;
+    const unsigned int pivotDestinationBase = pivotTile * TILE_SIZE + threadIdx.y;
+    unsigned int distance[OUTPUTS_PER_THREAD];
+    unsigned int pathValue[OUTPUTS_PER_THREAD];
+
+    #pragma unroll
+    for (unsigned int output = 0; output < OUTPUTS_PER_THREAD; ++output) {
+        const unsigned int row = threadIdx.y + output * THREAD_ROWS;
+        const unsigned int destination = destinationBase + output * THREAD_ROWS;
+        const unsigned int pivotDestination = pivotDestinationBase + output * THREAD_ROWS;
+        const size_t element = static_cast<size_t>(destination) * stride + source;
+        const size_t leftElement = static_cast<size_t>(pivotDestination) * stride + source;
+        const size_t rightElement = static_cast<size_t>(destination) * stride + pivotSource;
+        left[row][threadIdx.x] = dist[leftElement];
+        right[row][threadIdx.x] = dist[rightElement];
+        distance[output] = dist[element];
+        pathValue[output] = path[element];
+    }
+    __syncthreads();
+
+    #pragma unroll
+    for (unsigned int k = 0; k < TILE_SIZE; ++k) {
+        const unsigned int leftDistance = left[k][threadIdx.x];
+        #pragma unroll
+        for (unsigned int output = 0; output < OUTPUTS_PER_THREAD; ++output) {
+            const unsigned int row = threadIdx.y + output * THREAD_ROWS;
+            const unsigned int candidate = leftDistance + right[row][k];
+            if (candidate < distance[output]) {
+                distance[output] = candidate;
+                pathValue[output] = pivotTile * TILE_SIZE + k;
+            }
+        }
+    }
+
+    #pragma unroll
+    for (unsigned int output = 0; output < OUTPUTS_PER_THREAD; ++output) {
+        const unsigned int destination = destinationBase + output * THREAD_ROWS;
+        const size_t element = static_cast<size_t>(destination) * stride + source;
+        dist[element] = distance[output];
+        path[element] = pathValue[output];
+    }
+}
+
+float floydWarshallCuda(std::vector<unsigned int>& dist,
+                        std::vector<unsigned int>& path,
+                        const size_t numNodes) {
+    if (numNodes == 0) {
+        return 0.0F;
+    }
+
+    const size_t paddedNodes = ((numNodes + TILE_SIZE - 1) / TILE_SIZE) * TILE_SIZE;
+    if (paddedNodes > std::numeric_limits<unsigned int>::max()) {
+        std::fprintf(stderr, "CUDA Floyd-Warshall supports at most %u nodes\\n",
+                     std::numeric_limits<unsigned int>::max());
+        std::exit(EXIT_FAILURE);
+    }
+
+    const size_t paddedElements = paddedNodes * paddedNodes;
+    std::vector<unsigned int> paddedDist(paddedElements, INF);
+    std::vector<unsigned int> paddedPath(paddedElements, 0);
+    for (size_t destination = 0; destination < numNodes; ++destination) {
+        std::copy_n(dist.data() + destination * numNodes, numNodes,
+                    paddedDist.data() + destination * paddedNodes);
+        std::copy_n(path.data() + destination * numNodes, numNodes,
+                    paddedPath.data() + destination * paddedNodes);
+    }
+
+    unsigned int* deviceDist = nullptr;
+    unsigned int* devicePath = nullptr;
+    const size_t bytes = paddedElements * sizeof(unsigned int);
+    CUDA_CHECK(cudaMalloc(&deviceDist, bytes));
+    CUDA_CHECK(cudaMalloc(&devicePath, bytes));
+    CUDA_CHECK(cudaMemcpy(deviceDist, paddedDist.data(), bytes, cudaMemcpyHostToDevice));
+    CUDA_CHECK(cudaMemcpy(devicePath, paddedPath.data(), bytes, cudaMemcpyHostToDevice));
+
+    cudaEvent_t start{};
+    cudaEvent_t stop{};
+    CUDA_CHECK(cudaEventCreate(&start));
+    CUDA_CHECK(cudaEventCreate(&stop));
+
+    const unsigned int tileCount = static_cast<unsigned int>(paddedNodes / TILE_SIZE);
+    const dim3 threads(TILE_SIZE, THREAD_ROWS);
+    CUDA_CHECK(cudaEventRecord(start));
+    for (unsigned int pivotTile = 0; pivotTile < tileCount; ++pivotTile) {
+        floydPivotKernel<<<1, threads>>>(deviceDist, devicePath, paddedNodes, pivotTile);
+        CUDA_CHECK(cudaPeekAtLastError());
+
+        if (tileCount > 1) {
+            floydPivotRowColumnKernel<<<dim3(tileCount - 1, 2), threads>>>(
+                deviceDist, devicePath, paddedNodes, pivotTile);
+            CUDA_CHECK(cudaPeekAtLastError());
+            floydRemainingKernel<<<dim3(tileCount - 1, tileCount - 1), threads>>>(
+                deviceDist, devicePath, paddedNodes, pivotTile);
+            CUDA_CHECK(cudaPeekAtLastError());
+        }
+    }
+    CUDA_CHECK(cudaEventRecord(stop));
+    CUDA_CHECK(cudaEventSynchronize(stop));
+
+    float elapsedMilliseconds = 0.0F;
+    CUDA_CHECK(cudaEventElapsedTime(&elapsedMilliseconds, start, stop));
+
+    CUDA_CHECK(cudaMemcpy2D(dist.data(), numNodes * sizeof(unsigned int),
+                            deviceDist, paddedNodes * sizeof(unsigned int),
+                            numNodes * sizeof(unsigned int), numNodes,
+                            cudaMemcpyDeviceToHost));
+    CUDA_CHECK(cudaMemcpy2D(path.data(), numNodes * sizeof(unsigned int),
+                            devicePath, paddedNodes * sizeof(unsigned int),
+                            numNodes * sizeof(unsigned int), numNodes,
+                            cudaMemcpyDeviceToHost));
+
+    CUDA_CHECK(cudaEventDestroy(start));
+    CUDA_CHECK(cudaEventDestroy(stop));
+    CUDA_CHECK(cudaFree(deviceDist));
+    CUDA_CHECK(cudaFree(devicePath));
+    return elapsedMilliseconds;
+}
+
+}  // namespace
+
 // Index calculation for flattened 2D array
 inline constexpr size_t idx2(const size_t i, const size_t j, const size_t n) noexcept {
     return j * n + i;

 constexpr unsigned int INF = 1000000000;
 constexpr unsigned int MAX_DISTANCE = 200;
+constexpr unsigned int TILE_SIZE = 32;
+constexpr unsigned int THREAD_ROWS = 8;
+constexpr unsigned int OUTPUTS_PER_THREAD = TILE_SIZE / THREAD_ROWS;
+
+#define CUDA_CHECK(call)                                                        \
+    do {                                                                        \
+        const cudaError_t error_ = (call);                                     \
+        if (error_ != cudaSuccess) {                                           \
+            std::fprintf(stderr, "CUDA error at %s:%d: %s\\n", __FILE__,     \
+                         __LINE__, cudaGetErrorString(error_));                \
+            std::exit(EXIT_FAILURE);                                           \
